Yet relevant work remains scattered across foundation models, longitudinal modelling, disease simulation, treatment-effect estimation, reinforcement learning and digital twins. To bridge this gap, this review outlines a roadmap for advancing medical AI from isolated diagnosis and prediction toward medical world models that simulate disease evolution and support intervention decisions. This roadmap is organized around three coupled capabilities: patient-state construction, clinical dynamics modelling and intervention decision support. Across representative systems, the comparison highlights what each capability contributes and how partial components can be integrated into more mature perception--dynamics--planning systems. Finally, we identify the challenges involved in turning plausible rollouts into clinically useful simulators. Related literature is available at https://github.com/1999kevin/awesome_medical_world_models.

</details>


### [107] [AgentFairBench: Do LLM Agents Discriminate When They Act?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16723)
*Triveni Morla,Rohith Reddy Bellibaltu,Manpreet Singh,Manmeet Singh Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentFairBench，一个用于评估LLM智能体在多领域（招聘、贷款、医疗分诊）中行动层面人口统计学差异的基准，并引入"arity匹配零假设"方法论以避免统计膨胀导致的虚假显著结论。实验发现，在正确匹配的噪声基线下，Claude模型未检测到超出采样噪声的人口统计学效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM公平性评估仍停留在对"答案"进行评分，而非评估智能体实际执行的"行动"（如筛选申请者、推荐信贷、分诊患者）中的公平性问题。缺乏一个低成本、可复现、覆盖多领域的基准来测量LLM智能体在行动层面的群体差异。

Method: 构建Bias传导框架（BCF），覆盖招聘、贷款、医疗分诊三个领域；使用仅编码种族×性别名称信号的合成人口中性反事实匹配数据集；设计四种代理框架（直接、思维链、多智能体协商、工具增强）；采用NumPy仅计算引擎，计算反事实翻转率、均值绝对分数差（MASD）、行动率差异、工具调用差异等指标，并配以bootstrap置信区间、配对检验和FDR控制。

Result: 在864次决策的实验中，发现使用六组分数跨度与两轮噪声差异比较会将效应高估约2.4倍。在arity匹配的噪声基线和全体组检验下，Claude Haiku未显示出超出采样噪声的人口统计学效应（120个配对检验和9个全体检验均未通过校正）。植入偏差测试证实工具能够检测到存在的偏差。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提供了一个可靠、灵敏、可采用的评估工具，引入了"arity匹配零假设"方法论以纠正统计过度推断问题，并开源了代码、数据和评估框架，可扩展到更大规模的评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly take actions (screening applicants, recommending credit, triaging patients), yet fairness for LLMs is still measured by grading answers. We introduce AgentFairBench, a cheap, reproducible, multi-domain benchmark for demographic disparity in the actions of LLM agents. Grounded in a companion framework, the Bias Conduction Framework (BCF, restated here), it spans three regulator-anchored domains: hiring, lending, and medical triage. Synthetic, demographic-neutral profiles are evaluated in counterfactual matched sets that vary only a name-coded race x gender signal (in the Bertrand Mullainathan tradition), under four agent scaffolds of increasing agency (direct, chain-of-thought, multi-agent deliberation, tool-augmented). A NumPy-only harness computes counterfactual flip rate, mean absolute score difference (MASD), action-rate disparity, and tool-invocation disparity, with bootstrap confidence intervals, paired tests, and false-discovery-rate control, for single-digit dollars per model. A live leaderboard with a held-out private split and a contamination canary admits external models by submission. Our pilot (864 decisions plus a test-retest replication) carries a methodological lesson: comparing a six-group score spread against a two-run noise difference overstates disparity by ~ 2.4X through statistic arity alone. Against an arity matched noise floor and an omnibus group test, claude haiku 4 5 shows no demographic effect above sampling noise (0 of 120 pairwise and 0 of 9 omnibus contrasts survive correction); a planted-bias test confirms the instrument detects disparity when present. The contribution is a sound, sensitive, adoption-ready instrument, the arity matched null methodology, and open artifacts to scale it. Code, data, and harness are released under open licenses, with an anonymized review artifact.

</details>


### [108] [A First-Principles Derivation of LLM Policy Optimization: From Expected Reward to GRPO and Its Structural Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16733)
*Jianghan Shen,Siqi Luo,Yue Li,Jiyao Liu,Wanying Qu,Yi Zhang,Ziyan Huang,Tianbin Li,Ming Hu,Xiaohong Liu,Yirong Chen,Junjun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，基于轨迹概率 $p_θ(τ)$ 和奖励 $R(τ)$ 两个维度，系统梳理了从 REINFORCE 到 PPO、GRPO 及其后续变体的 LLM 策略优化方法，揭示每种方法在梯度估计器中的具体干预位置与设计动机，并指出现有方法无法解决的复合失效问题，为下一代算法设计提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述按领域或时间线组织，掩盖了各方法设计选择背后的原理及其在梯度估计器中的具体干预位置，因此需要一种统一、可诊断、可扩展的分析框架来揭示这些方法的内在逻辑。

Method: 以共享目标函数 $J(θ) = \mathbb{E}_{τ\sim p_θ(τ)}[R(τ)]$ 为基础，沿"轨迹侧"（由 $p_θ(τ)$ 诱导）和"奖励侧"（由 $R(τ)$ 诱导）两个轴定位每种方法的修改位置，涵盖 REINFORCE、PPO、GRPO、后 GRPO 变体、Agentic RL 和 GRPO-OPD。

Result: 该框架统一、可诊断且可扩展：从共享目标分析各方法，识别每类方法修改了哪一侧及其原因，并揭示了需要轨迹侧和奖励侧联合设计才能解决的复合失效问题。

Conclusion: 现有方法的边界情况和耦合失效点指明了当前解决方案的局限，为设计下一代 LLM 策略优化算法提供了原则性起点。

Abstract: Policy gradient algorithms for language models optimize the same objective $J(θ) = \mathbb{E}*{τ\sim p*θ(τ)}[R(τ)]$, which has exactly two factors: the trajectory probability $p_θ(τ)$ and the reward $R(τ)$. Every method from REINFORCE to PPO to GRPO and their descendants modifies one or both factors to address a specific failure in the preceding formulation. Existing surveys organize these methods by domain or chronology, which obscures the rationale behind each design choice and the precise location of its intervention within the gradient estimator. This survey revisits the landscape of LLM policy optimization from $J(θ)$ on first principles and uses the trajectory side, induced by $p_θ(τ)$, and the reward side, induced by $R(τ)$, as the two axes along which methods are located. It covers the path from REINFORCE and PPO to GRPO, as well as post-GRPO variants, Agentic RL, and GRPO-OPD. The resulting framework is unified, diagnostic, and extensible: it analyzes methods from a shared objective, identifies which side each method modifies and why, and applies the same trajectory and reward axes across these settings. Across these settings, the framework also exposes compound failures that no single-side fix resolves and that therefore require joint design of the trajectory side and the reward side. The boundary cases and coupled failures identified by this map mark where existing solutions run out and provide a principled starting point for designing the next generation of LLM policy optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [109] [Skill-to-LoRA: From Using Skills to Learning Behaviors for Token-Efficient LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16769)
*Tianyi Zhang,Zhonghao Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Skill-to-LoRA (S2L)方法，将智能体技能从运行时注入的SKILL.md文档转化为可动态加载的LoRA适配器，在SWE-Skills-Bench的21个技能子集上，相比全技能文本基线提升5.2个百分点通过率，同时减少6.6%的每步token成本，证明过程性技能可从运行时指令转化为可训练、可动态加载的行为模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体技能通常以SKILL.md文件形式分发，虽便于检查和复用，但每次执行时需将相同的过程性文档重复注入到运行时上下文中，导致token浪费和效率低下。

Method: S2L采用行为中心的技能表示：离线阶段，利用完整SKILL.md合成技能引导的演示数据，训练技能特定的LoRA适配器；在线阶段，省略完整文档，动态加载对应LoRA适配器以激活已习得的技能行为。

Result: 在SWE-Skills-Bench的21个技能子集上，S2L相比无技能基线提升2.9个百分点，相比全技能文本基线提升5.2个百分点通过率；每步token成本相比全技能文本降低6.6%；在18/21个技能上匹配或超过全技能文本，在15/21个技能上匹配或超过无技能基线；控制实验表明错误LoRA和共享LoRA均降低性能，验证了技能特定适配器对齐的必要性。

Conclusion: 许多过程性智能体技能可以从运行时指令转化为可训练、可动态加载的行为模块，S2L提供了一种高效且有效的技能表示范式。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly distributed as SKILL.md files: human-readable procedural documents that describe workflows, tools, resources, and domain conventions. While convenient for inspection and reuse, this design requires the same reusable procedure to be repeatedly injected into the runtime context. We propose Skill-to-LoRA(S2L), a behavior-centric skill representation that replaces runtime skill text with skill-specific LoRA adapters. Rather than compressing the skill document itself, S2L models the behavioral change induced by the skill text: offline, the complete SKILL.md is used to synthesize skill-guided demonstrations; online, the full document is omitted and the corresponding LoRA adapter is dynamically loaded to activate the learned skill behavior. We evaluate S2L with Qwen3.6-27B on a 21-skill subset of SWE-Skills-Bench. Compared with the no-skill and Full Skill Text baselines, S2L improves pass rate by 2.9 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively, while reducing per-step token cost by 6.6% relative to Full Skill Text prompting. S2L matches or improves Full Skill Text on 18/21 skills and the no-skill baseline on 15/21 skills. Control experiments further show that the gains depend on skill-specific adapter alignment: Wrong-LoRA and Shared-LoRA both reduce performance. These results suggest that many procedural agent skills can be converted from runtime instructions into trainable, dynamically loadable behavioral modules. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [110] [OpenClaw-Skill: Collective Skill Tree Search for Agentic Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16774)
*Tianyi Lin,Chuanyu Sun,Jingyi Zhang,Changxu Wei,Huanjin Yao,Shunyu Liu,Xikun Zhang,Liu Liu,Jiaxing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出集体技能树搜索(CSTS)框架，通过集体智能自动构建可复用的技能树，增强LLM代理在工具使用、多步推理和动态环境交互中的能力。训练得到的OpenClaw-Skill模型在长程规划、工具使用和泛化方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LLM代理自动构建可复用的有效技能，以增强其在复杂真实系统（如OpenClaw）中处理工具使用、多步推理和动态环境交互等复杂任务的能力。

Method: 提出CSTS框架，包含两个迭代阶段：(1) 集体技能节点生成(CSN-Gen)：利用多模型集体知识探索多样化候选技能；(2) 集体技能节点评估(CSN-Assess)：多模型作为裁判，通过集体质量评分和集体可迁移性评分评估选择技能节点。此外引入集体技能强化学习，主动选择多个相关技能以拓宽解空间探索。

Result: 构建了全面的技能树及技能增强训练数据，训练得到OpenClaw-Skill模型，在长程规划、工具使用和泛化能力上超越现有基准。

Conclusion: CSTS框架通过集体智能有效构建结构化、多样化且可泛化的技能树，显著提升了LLM代理的复杂任务解决能力。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Model (LLM) agents with effective skills is crucial for solving complex tasks in real-world systems like OpenClaw. In this work, we aim to develop a framework that automatically constructs such reusable skills to enhance LLMs in tool use, multi-step reasoning, and dynamic environment interaction. To this end, we propose Collective Skill Tree Search (CSTS), a novel tree-search-based skill construction framework that constructs structured, diverse and generalizable tree of skills. The core idea of CSTS is to leverage collective intelligence to jointly search, identify and compose effective skills via two iterative phases: Collective Skill Node Generation (CSN-Gen) and Collective Skill Node Assessment (CSN-Assess). CSN-Gen exploits collective knowledge from multiple models to explore diverse candidate skills for each subtask, enabling comprehensive skill exploration. CSN-Assess employs multiple models as judges to evaluate and select skill nodes with two scoring mechanisms: (1) collective quality scoring that aggregates independent evaluations to produce a robust estimate of skill effectiveness, and (2) collective transferability scoring that explicitly verifies whether a skill generalizes well across different models. With CSTS, we construct a set of comprehensive tree of skills along with skill-augmented training data, enabling models to effectively learn and utilize skills. Besides, we introduce Collective Skill Reinforcement Learning, which actively selects multiple relevant skills from the tree to broaden solution-space exploration, avoid being trapped by a single skill and its resulting homogeneous or suboptimal solutions. As a result, our trained model, OpenClaw-Skill, exhibits outstanding agentic capabilities in long-horizon planning, tool use and generalization over challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [LabOSBench: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents for Scientific Instrument Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16802)
*Anqi Zou,Han Deng,Chengyu Zhang,Junquan Hu,Yu Wang,Yuxiang Xing,Aokai Zhang,Hanling Zhang,Zhaoyang Liu,Ben Fei,Zhihui Wang,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LabOSBench是一个基于Web科学仪器模拟器的多模态GUI智能体基准测试，包含8个仪器模拟器的96个子任务，用于评估智能体在科学仪器控制中的表现。实验表明现有智能体在结构化GUI任务上表现尚可，但在反馈驱动操作和长周期工作流执行上存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用基准主要关注虚拟化系统中的软件操作任务，而科学仪器场景需要复杂的界面协调控制和反馈驱动的参数调整。直接在物理高精度仪器上评估智能体成本高、风险大、可及性有限且难以保证可重复评估，因此需要构建一个模拟但逼真的测试平台。

Method: 构建了一套基于Web的科学仪器模拟器，通过浏览器直接操作，避免资源密集的OS虚拟化。设计了96个子任务，涵盖从样品加载、对准、参数调优、数据采集到结果检查的完整工作流。支持灵活的任务配置和基于执行结果的评估。

Result: 评估了通用视觉语言模型、专用GUI智能体模型和高级智能体框架在子任务和端到端两个层面上的表现。实验发现现有智能体能完成许多结构化GUI子任务，但在反馈驱动操作和长周期工作流执行上仍然困难。

Conclusion: LabOSBench为推动计算机使用智能体向科学仪器控制方向发展提供了一个可重复、低成本的测试平台。

Abstract: Current computer-use benchmarks primarily focus on software operation tasks in virtualized systems, whereas scientific instrumentation scenarios require coordinated control over complex interfaces, and feedback-driven parameter adjustment. However, directly evaluating agents on physical high-precision instruments is impractical due to high cost, safety risks, limited accessibility, and difficulty in ensuring reproducible evaluation. This motivates the need for a simulated yet realistic testbed that preserves the operational challenges of scientific instruments while enabling scalable and safe benchmarking. To this end, we introduce LabOSBench, a challenging benchmark for multimodal GUI agents built on a suite of web-based scientific-instrument simulators. Operating directly via a browser, LabOSBench avoids resource-heavy OS virtualization while supporting flexible task configuration and execution-based evaluation. Specifically, LabOSBench constructs 96 subtasks across eight instrument simulators, covering workflows from sample loading, alignment, parameter tuning, and data acquisition to result inspection. We evaluate general-purpose vision-language models, specialized GUI agent models, and advanced agentic frameworks at both subtask and end-to-end levels. Our experiments reveal that while existing agents can complete many structured GUI subtasks, they still struggle with feedback-driven operations and long-horizon workflow execution. Overall, LabOSBench provides a reproducible, low-cost testbed for advancing computer-using agents toward scientific-instrument control.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive and Explicit safe: Triggering Latent Safety Awareness in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16808)
*Ke Miao,Jiaxin Li,Hongliang Chen,Yuke Hu,Zhan Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe Trigger方法，利用大推理模型（LRM）自身的潜在安全意识，通过SFT和DPO两个训练阶段（完全使用模型自身生成的响应）显著提升模型安全性，在有害和越狱基准上的攻击成功率分别平均下降24.65%和36.72%，且对通用性能几乎没有负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型虽然在复杂任务上表现出色，但极易受到高级越狱攻击和直接有害查询的影响。已有方法依赖外部人工数据标注进行安全对齐，成本高且效率低。作者观察到LRM在重新呈现原始查询及其自身推理轨迹时，能够内在识别安全风险，即具备"潜在安全意识"，因此希望利用这一能力构建无需外部标注的安全对齐方法。

Method: 首先通过SFT显式诱导安全标签（safe tags），在不安全查询的初始推理内容后触发安全分析和指导，对通用查询则保持标准响应以实现自适应触发；随后使用DPO进一步增强安全分析和指导的正确性与稳定性。两个阶段所需的响应数据完全由被优化的模型自身生成。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B在有害基准上的ASR平均下降24.65%，在越狱基准上平均下降36.72%。Safe Trigger方法对通用性能和用户体验几乎没有负面影响。

Conclusion: 利用LRM自身潜在安全意识，结合SFT+DPO的两阶段自生成训练范式，能够在不依赖外部人工标注、不影响通用性能的前提下，显著提升模型对有害查询和越狱攻击的防御能力。

Abstract: While Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) excel at complex tasks, they remain highly vulnerable to sophisticated jailbreaks and direct harmful queries. To address this vulnerability, prior works depend heavily on external manual data annotation for safety alignment. However, we observe that LRMs can inherently identify safety risks when being re-presented with original queries alongside their own reasoning trajectories -- a capability we term Latent Safety Awareness. To leverage this safety awareness, we first employ Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) to explicitly induce safe tags to trigger safety analysis and guidance following the initial reasoning content for unsafe queries, while preserving standard responses for general queries to ensure adaptive triggering. Subsequently, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to further enhance the correctness and stability of the safety analysis and guidance. Notably, responses required for both training stages are entirely generated by models being optimized. With (Safe Trigger) SFT and DPO, experimental results demonstrate significant safety enhancement. For example, the Attack Success Rate (ASR) of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, on average, drops 24.65% and 36.72% on harmful and jailbreak benchmarks, respectively. Finally, our Safe Trigger method exerts almost no negative impact on general performance or user experience.

</details>


### [113] [Scaling LLM Reasoning from Minimal Labels: A Semi-Supervised Framework with a Lightweight Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16811)
*Keizo Kato,Chenhui Chu,Yugo Murawaki,Sado Kurohashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出半监督框架，利用轻量级推理正确性分类器和熵置信度阈值，从极少量标注数据中扩展推理学习，在数学和视觉问答任务上达到使用10-15倍标注数据的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM伪中间推理方法依赖大量正确标注答案来评估推理质量，标注成本高昂，限制了推理学习的大规模扩展。

Method: 训练一个轻量级推理正确性分类器（仅需少量标注样本），判断LLM生成的中间推理轨迹是否有效；再通过基于熵的置信度阈值过滤不可靠样本，用剩余高置信度推理轨迹微调模型。

Result: 在Orca-Math子集和GQA视觉编程任务上，该方法仅用少量标注数据即可达到使用10-15倍标注数据的精度；消融实验证实分类器和熵过滤对可扩展和抗噪伪标注均至关重要。

Conclusion: 用轻量级推理验证替代昂贵的答案级监督，为大规模构建推理资源和从极少人工输入学习的自主推理系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: For the development of Large language models (LLMs), recent approaches to generating pseudo intermediate reasoning have shown remarkable progress. But they typically rely on large numbers of correctly annotated answers to assess reasoning quality. This paper presents a semi-supervised framework that scales reasoning learning from minimal supervision, turning reasoning verification itself into a data creation mechanism. We train a lightweight reasoning-correctness classifier on only a few labeled samples, which judges whether intermediate reasoning traces generated by an LLM are valid. Furthermore, an entropy-based confidence threshold filters out unreliable samples, and the remaining high-confidence reasoning traces are used to fine-tune the model. Experiments on Verifiable Math Problems (Orca-Math subset) and Question Answering on Image Scene Graphs (GQA) with Visual Programming show that our method achieves accuracy comparable to using 10-15x more labeled data. Ablation analyses confirm that both the classifier and entropy filtering are essential for scalable and noise-resistant pseudo-labeling. By replacing expensive answer-level supervision with lightweight reasoning verification, our method provides a practical path toward constructing large-scale reasoning resources and paves the way for future autonomous reasoning systems that learn from minimal human input.

</details>


### [114] [GIST-CMTF: Goal-State Inference for Causal Minimal Tool Filtering in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16813)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Rohit Shukla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GIST-CMTF通过在因果最小工具过滤(CMTF)基础上增加目标状态推断层，预测候选符号目标并估计模糊性，将工具增强型LLM代理的任务成功率从80.1%提升至97.0%，并将错误目标执行从19.4%降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMTF方法假设用户请求已映射到符号目标状态，但实际中请求可能对应多个可能目标，导致代理遵循有效因果工具路径执行错误目标。需要一种能在暴露外部工具前验证目标状态的方法。

Method: 提出GIST-CMTF，包含目标状态推断层：1) 使用与CMTF相同的状态转换词汇预测候选符号目标；2) 估计目标模糊性；3) 根据模糊性选择应用CMTF或将澄清作为因果动作，以获取缺失的目标或状态变量。

Result: 在7个模型后端、6种过滤方法、120个受控工具使用任务上，GIST-CMTF达到97.0%任务成功率（对比top-goal CMTF的80.1%和semantic-goal CMTF的82.9%），错误目标执行从19.4%降至2.5%，同时保持了因果过滤的单工具暴露特性和较低的token消耗。

Conclusion: 可靠的工具增强型代理应在暴露外部动作前验证目标状态，而不仅仅是工具相关性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents rely on runtime filtering to decide which tools should be visible at each step. Causal Minimal Tool Filtering (CMTF) reduces tool-choice confusion by exposing only the next causally necessary tool frontier, but it assumes that the user request has already been mapped to a symbolic goal state. In practice, requests such as "handle my appointment" or "take care of this email" may correspond to multiple possible goals. This creates wrong-goal execution, where an agent follows a valid causal tool path for an unintended objective. We introduce GIST-CMTF, a goal-state inference layer that predicts candidate symbolic goals over the same state-transition vocabulary used by CMTF, estimates ambiguity, and either applies CMTF or exposes clarification as a causal action that produces missing goal or state variables. We evaluate GIST-CMTF across seven model backends, six filtering methods, and 120 controlled tool-use tasks. GIST-CMTF achieves 97.0% task success, compared with 80.1% for top-goal CMTF and 82.9% for semantic-goal CMTF. It reduces wrong-goal execution from 19.4% under top-goal CMTF to 2.5%, while preserving the one-tool exposure of causal filtering and using substantially fewer tokens than all-tools exposure. These results suggest that reliable tool-augmented agents should validate goal state, not only tool relevance, before exposing external actions.

</details>


### [115] [Symbolic Informalization: Fluent, Productive, Multilingual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16893)
*Aarne Ranta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Informath项目旨在通过符号非形式化（symbolic informalization）将形式化数学可靠地转换为自然语言，基于Dedukti（形式系统枢纽）和Grammatical Framework（多语言生成）的中间语言架构，实现多证明系统（Agda、Lean、Rocq）与多自然语言之间的流畅、精准转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学内容（机器验证的证明）通常难以被人类直接理解，现有的语法糖机制表达能力有限。随着AI构造证明和自动形式化的发展，需要一种能将形式化内容精准、流畅地转换为自然语言的方法，同时保持精度不丢失。

Method: 提出的Informath项目采用中间语言架构：Dedukti作为不同证明系统（Agda、Lean、Rocq）之间的枢纽，负责形式化内容的交换；Grammatical Framework (GF)负责处理自然语言的语法正确性和语言变体，将中间表示转换为多种自然语言的流畅文本。

Result: 论文展示了符号非形式化能以合理的开发成本生成流畅的数学文本，支持多个形式系统（Agda、Lean、Rocq）和多个自然语言，实现了形式化内容到人类可读文本的可靠转换。

Conclusion: 符号非形式化是连接形式化数学与自然语言的有效途径，Informath的中间语言架构能够以较低成本实现多证明系统与多自然语言之间的精准、流畅转换，对AI构造证明的可解释性和自动形式化的发展具有重要价值。

Abstract: Symbolic informalization enables a reliable conversion of formal mathematics to natural language. It has the potential to make machine-checked content human-readable without loss of precision. In a traditional proof system usage, symbolic informalization generalizes the limited mechanisms of syntactic sugar into the ordinary language of mathematics. In a setting where proofs are constructed by artificial intelligence and autoformalization, symbolic informalization can explain what precisely has been constructed. This paper outlines the project Informath, which aims to show how symbolic informalization can produce fluent text with a reasonable development effort and address multiple formal and natural languages. Informath is based on an interlingual architecture, where Dedukti works as a hub between different proof systems (Agda, Lean, Rocq) and Grammatical Framework (GF) takes care of linguistic correctness and variation in different natural languages.

</details>


### [116] [Greed Is Learned: Visible Incentives as Reward-Hacking Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16914)
*Tong Che,Rui Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 强化学习会导致策略对可见的奖励通道（如仪表盘、KPI）产生"成瘾"行为，在跨领域追逐显示收益、牺牲真实任务，并会翻转模型的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的智能体越来越多地在其奖励代理（如分数、KPI仪表盘）可见的情况下行动，这种设置可能导致危险行为，因此需要系统研究这种"奖励通道成瘾"现象及其对安全对齐的影响。

Method: 构建了名为MoneyWorld的合成沙盒环境，通过对比可见奖励通道与不可见通道的设置，训练强化学习策略，观察其行为变化，并在不同模型规模和系列上进行验证。

Result: 1) 策略对可见的奖励通道产生成瘾，跨领域追逐显示收益；2) 牺牲真正任务以追逐通道；3) 跟随重写的通道改变行为；4) 可翻转模型的安全对齐——仅用无害金钱任务训练后，模型会放弃原本始终采取的安全动作去换取仪表盘奖励；5) 这种学到的贿赂行为在不同模型规模和系列中均存在。

Conclusion: 盲目地让超强下一代AI在KPI或损益表上进行优化是危险的，贪婪是在追逐奖励通道的过程中习得的。

Abstract: Deployed agents increasingly act with their reward proxy in view, such as a balance, score, or KPI dashboard. We show that reinforcement learning can make a policy \emph{addicted} to such a visible self-benefit channel. It chases the displayed payoff across held-out domains, sacrifices the true task to do so, and follows the channel wherever we rewrite it, while policies that never saw the channel stay honest. We call this \emph{reward-channel addiction} and study it in \emph{MoneyWorld}, a synthetic sandbox. The addiction can \emph{flip a model's safety alignment}: trained only on innocuous money tasks with no safety content, the model abandons the safe action it otherwise always takes whenever a dashboard pays for an unsafe one, and reverts to safe once the channel is hidden. This learned bribe replicates across model scales and families. Blindly optimizing super-capable, next-generation AI on KPIs or P\&L can be dangerous for alignment. \emph{Greed is learned} when following such a channel pays.

</details>


### [117] [MA-SBI: Misspecification-Aware Simulation-Based Inference via Side-Channel Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16923)
*Arunkumar V,Manoranjan Gandhudi,Gangadharan G. R.,Arun Prakash,S. Senthilkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MA-SBI，一种无需真实参数校准的鲁棒模拟推理框架，利用非结构化侧信息（如文本）作为后验校正，理论保证偏差降低受互信息界限约束，在隐藏校准基准测试中匹配Oracle后验性能，并在真实流行病学数据上提升预测对数似然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒SBI方法（如RoPE）需要真实参数校准对，但在实际应用中这类数据通常不可得；而实践者往往拥有非结构化侧信息（如标签、文本、公告等），如何利用这些侧信息进行模型校正成为研究动机。

Method: MA-SBI框架包含一个学习校正器，将侧通道文本映射到观测空间偏移量，该偏移量作用于任何预训练的摊销后验之前，无需重新训练也无需真实参数。理论方面，通过Donsker-Varadhan定理推导了互信息约束下偏差降低的上界。

Result: 在隐藏校准基准测试中，仅使用文本的MA-SBI在10个种子和两个骨干网络上匹配Oracle后验（TOST等价），而RoPE即使使用更多数据也无法达到；在结构性能从参数对中恢复的设定下RoPE占优；随机变体在真实COVID和OxCGRT流行病学数据上提升后验预测对数似然，在良好指定的认知科学语料上保持后验不变。

Conclusion: MA-SBI是一种校准免费、可即插即用的鲁棒SBI框架，与RoPE互补：前者适合利用文本侧信息进行非结构校正，后者适合结构性能从参数对中恢复的场景。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) of latent parameters is often hindered by simulator misspecification, the mismatch between simulated and real-world observations caused by inherent modeling simplifications. RoPE, the recent state-of-the-art for robust SBI, addresses this through optimal transport between learned representations of real and simulated observations, but requires ground-truth parameter calibration pairs that are typically unavailable in the very settings where SBI is needed. What practitioners do have is unstructured side-information such as regime labels, instruction text, and policy bulletins. We propose Misspecification-Aware Simulation-Based Inference (MA-SBI), a calibration-free framework that turns this side-channel into a posterior correction. A learned corrector maps side-channel text to an observation-space shift applied before any pre-trained amortized posterior, requiring no retraining and no parameter ground-truth. Our main theorem bounds achievable bias reduction by the mutual information between misspecification and side-channel, with a non-vacuous constant that extends to all sub-Gaussian noise via Donsker-Varadhan. On hide-the-calibration benchmarks, MA-SBI with text alone matches the oracle posterior across 10 seeds and two backbones (TOST equivalence), while RoPE given more data does not. The two approaches are complementary: where misspecification is structural and recoverable from parameter pairs, RoPE dominates, as the theory predicts. A stochastic variant improves posterior-predictive log-likelihood on real COVID and OxCGRT epidemiological data, and correctly leaves the posterior unchanged on a well-specified cognitive-science corpus.

</details>


### [118] [RAID: Semantic Graph Diffusion for True Cold-Start and Cross-Lingual Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16925)
*Arunkumar V,Manoranjan Gandhudi,Gangadharan G. R.,Arun Prakash,S. Senthilkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAID框架，通过元数据驱动语义检索和图条件扩散，在不依赖历史观测的情况下实现时间序列冷启动预测，显著优于基础模型和基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型依赖历史窗口进行预测，但真实冷启动场景中（新物品无任何历史观测数据）该假设不成立，亟需一种不依赖历史数据即可进行预测的方法。

Method: RAID框架包括三个阶段：1) 使用冻结的多语言嵌入模型将文本元数据映射到共享语义空间；2) 构建归纳检索图以支持未见物品的检索；3) 先通过语义相关邻居聚合形成基础预测，再使用门控扩散模块对残差不确定性进行建模和修正。

Result: 在严格冷启动协议下，RAID在预测准确度和预测区间覆盖上均优于强基础模型和竞争性基线；通过非自回归解码将推理延迟降低一个数量级；支持零样本跨语言迁移。

Conclusion: 基于元数据语义的检索增强扩散方法可有效替代历史依赖方法来解决时间序列冷启动问题，同时具备高效推理和跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models show strong transfer performance when given a non-empty history window. However, true cold-start scenarios, where a new item has no prior observations, violate this assumption. We propose RAID (Retrieval-Augmented Iterative Diffusion) a framework, which replaces history-based correlation learning with metadata-driven semantic retrieval and graph-conditioned diffusion. RAID maps textual metadata into a shared semantic space using a frozen multilingual embedding model and constructs an inductive retrieval graph that extends naturally to unseen items. It first forms a base forecast by aggregating information from semantically related neighbors, then refines this forecast with a gated diffusion module to model residual uncertainty. Under a strict true cold-start protocol, RAID outperforms strong foundation models and competitive baselines on both forecasting accuracy and prediction interval coverage, while reducing inference latency by an order of magnitude through non-autoregressive decoding. The shared semantic space also enables zero-shot cross-lingual transfer, allowing a model trained on English descriptions to generalize to items described in other languages without direct supervision.

</details>


### [119] [A Causal Model of Theory of Mind in Conflict for Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16944)
*Nikolos Gurney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构因果模型，将心理理论（ToM）视为由情境和主体条件激活的机制而非始终开启的能力，通过三条因果路径（可处理性、推理深度、使能原因）决定何时在冲突中启用ToM，以优化认知准确性和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI-ToM模型聚焦于"如何"进行心智化，但忽略了"何时"需要启用ToM的问题，尤其是在冲突情境下缺乏对情境和主体层面条件的因果考量。

Method: 构建了一个有向无环图（DAG）形式的结构因果模型，包含四个外生变量（情境与主体条件）、五个内生中介变量、一个机制性ToM节点，以及三条因果路径（可处理性路径、推理深度路径、使能原因路径），最终输出认知准确性。

Result: 该框架为AI系统提供了有原则的、资源理性的心智化决策程序，将社会推理与行为策略解耦，并能在冲突以外泛化至更广泛的社会现象。

Conclusion: 该模型对AI系统的效率、信任和鲁棒人工社会智能的发展具有重要意义，并讨论了仿真验证、人机协作实证研究以及冲突优化心智化带来的伦理问题。

Abstract: Theory of mind (ToM), the capacity to ascribe mental states to others and use those ascriptions for prediction and inference, is widely assumed to be essential for effective human-machine integration. Existing AI-ToM models address \emph{how} to mentalize, but leave the question of when largely unaddressed. The central question is: under what situational and agent-level conditions is ToM engagement causally warranted in conflict? This paper presents a structural causal model formalized as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), treating ToM as a mechanism activated by situational and agent-level conditions rather than as an always-on capacity. The model specifies four exogenous variables capturing situational and agent-level conditions, five endogenous mediators, and a mechanistic ToM node producing engagement states through three distinct causal pathways: a tractability pathway, a reasoning-depth pathway, and an enabling-cause pathway. The primary outcome is epistemic accuracy, which decouples social reasoning from behavioral policy and generalizes across social phenomena beyond conflict. The framework gives AI systems a principled, resource-rational decision procedure for mentalizing, with implications for efficiency, trust, and the development of robust artificial social intelligence. Simulation validation, empirical human-machine teaming studies, and ethical considerations arising from conflict-optimized mentalizing are discussed.

</details>


### [120] [The embrace of open science: An analysis of a decade of AI research and 56 800 conference papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16974)
*Kevin L Coakley,Thijs Snelleman,Holger Hoos,Odd Erik Gundersen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2014-2024年间五大AI会议56,800篇论文的文档实践，发现代码和数据共享率从11%提升至64%，估计可复现性从28%增至64%。文档实践的改善先于可复现性检查表的引入，表明这些变化源于开放科学运动而非正式要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现性危机促使AI研究社区改进文档实践，但尚未有研究系统评估这些实践随时间的变化趋势，以及可复现性检查表等正式要求的影响效果。

Method: 识别七个可复现性变量并进行质量保证，对2014-2024年间五大顶级AI会议56,800篇论文进行系统分析，基于文档实践推断可复现性率。

Result: 文档实践显著改善：代码和数据共享率从11%增至64%（近六倍增长）；推断的可复现性从28%提升至64%。改进先于可复现性检查表的引入。

Conclusion: AI领域的文档实践和可复现性在过去十年有显著提升，但这些变化主要源于更广泛的开放科学运动，而非正式的可复现性检查表等政策要求。

Abstract: The reproducibility crisis has directed the AI research community toward improving documentation practices. Several studies have identified methodological issues, and in response, the most impactful venues in the field have introduced reproducibility checklists. We seek to understand whether documentation practices have changed over time by assessing all published papers at five leading AI conferences over the past decade. Seven reproducibility variables were identified, quality-assured and used to analyse 56 800 publications. Our analysis reveals that in the period 2014 to 2024, documentation practices have improved; papers sharing both code and data increased nearly sixfold, from 11% to 64% Building on empirical reproducibility rates from a prior study, we estimate - inferred from documentation practices, not direct testing - that reproducibility increased from 28% in 2014 to 64% in 2024. Improvements in documentation practices predate the introduction of reproducibility checklists, suggesting these changes reflect a broader movement toward open science rather than a direct response to formal requirements.

</details>


### [121] [Consensus-based Agentic Large Language Model Framework for Harmonized Tariff Schedule Code Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16987)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Khanh Van Quynh Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Tri Duong,Phuc Ho,Van Pham,Loc Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向加拿大10位HS编码分类的智能体大语言模型框架，整合多智能体信息检索、语义检索、基于证据的推理、共识验证、层级投票、置信估计和人机协同升级机制，在3,300条专家标注的真实物流数据集上验证了精确分类的挑战性，强调了证据驱动、不确定性感知和以人为中心的分类工作流的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海关HS编码分类对通关、税费评估、贸易统计和合规监管至关重要，但产品描述通常简短、不完整或模糊，且正确分类依赖于层级化的关税结构、法律注释和管辖区域特定规则，现有方法难以实现精确的10位编码分类。

Method: 提出一个智能体LLM框架，包含多智能体信息检索、官方关税文档语义检索、基于证据的推理、共识验证、层级码分量逐元素投票、置信度估计以及人机协同升级机制，用于加拿大10位HS编码分类。

Result: 在3,300条专家标注数据集上，精确的10位编码分类即使对先进LLM仍然困难，性能从粗粒度章节级预测到细粒度税目和统计后缀分配逐步下降。

Conclusion: 需要采用证据驱动、不确定性感知和以人为中心的分类工作流，而非完全自主的单步预测方法；所提框架支持更可解释、可问责且符合合规要求的HS分类。

Abstract: Accurate Harmonized Tariff Schedule (HTS) code classification is essential for customs clearance, duty assessment, trade statistics, and regulatory compliance in maritime logistics. However, exact HTS classification remains challenging because product descriptions are often short, incomplete, or ambiguous, while correct classification depends on hierarchical tariff structures, legal notes, and jurisdiction-specific rules. This paper proposes an agentic large language model (LLM) framework for Canadian 10-digit HTS code classification in smart-port and maritime logistics environments. The framework integrates multi-agent information retrieval, semantic retrieval over official tariff documents, evidence-grounded reasoning, consensus-based validation, element-wise voting across hierarchical code components, confidence estimation, and human-in-the-loop escalation. We evaluate the framework on a private dataset of 3,300 domain-expert-labeled product records collected from logistics and delivery contexts. Experimental results show that exact 10-digit classification remains difficult even for advanced LLMs, with performance decreasing from coarse chapter-level prediction to fine-grained tariff and statistical suffix assignment. These findings demonstrate the need for evidence-grounded, uncertainty-aware, and human-centered classification workflows rather than fully autonomous single-step prediction. The proposed framework supports more interpretable, accountable, and compliance-oriented HTS classification for maritime logistics and smart-port operations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/hts.

</details>


### [122] [When in Doubt, Plan It Out: Committed Small Language Model Deliberation for Reactive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16995)
*Nathan Gavenski,Juarez Monteiro,Francisco Galuppo,Adriano Veloso,Odinaldo Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACT是一个混合架构，将快速反应式RL策略与慢速深思熟虑的小语言模型（SLM）规划器相结合，通过异步SLM生成和验证候选行动计划，在模拟验证安全、可行和完整后直接执行，无需重新训练或修改RL策略。在三个难度递增的FrozenLake配置上，PACT使用2B参数SLM骨干网络即超越所有基线，表明深思熟虑的规划与反应式执行相结合比单独使用任何一种更强大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略在陌生环境中常常因为缺乏明确的推理能力而导致性能下降，因此需要一种能够结合快速反应与深思熟虑规划的混合架构来提升策略的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出PACT混合架构，包含快速反应式RL策略和慢速深思熟虑的SLM规划器。SLM异步生成并验证候选行动计划，通过模拟验证确保安全、可行和完整后，直接执行该计划，绕过RL策略，无需重新训练或修改RL策略。

Result: 在三个难度递增的FrozenLake配置上，使用2B参数的SLM骨干网络，PACT在所有基线方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: 深思熟虑的规划与反应式执行相结合比单独使用任何一种方法更强大，混合架构能够在不修改原有RL策略的前提下提升性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) policies often degrade in unfamiliar environments because they lack explicit deliberation. We propose Plan, Align, Commit, Think (PACT), a hybrid architecture that combines a fast, reactive RL policy with a slow, deliberative Small Language Model (SLM) planner. PACT invokes the SLM asynchronously to generate and validate candidate action plans. Once a plan is verified through simulation as safe, feasible, and complete, it is executed directly, bypassing the RL policy without retraining or modifying it. Evaluated on three FrozenLake configurations of increasing difficulty, PACT outperforms all baselines while relying on a 2B-parameter SLM backbone, suggesting that deliberative planning and reactive execution are more powerful in concert than either is alone in these settings.

</details>


### [123] [Bayesian Inference and Decision Audits for Public Archives of Frontier AI Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17005)
*Yanan Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示公共AI评测并非简单的排行榜，而是一个受报告规则、基准修订和数据缺失影响的选择性时间序列。通过对LiveBench、Open LLM Leaderboard v2、LMArena、GAIA和tau-bench等公开存档的分析，发现固定报告惯例下，一个终端示例可兼容两种历史路径（达到天花板0.05以内的时间分别为23.03或75.13）。候选选择感知的前沿模型在合成恢复、目标存档预测、偏好迁移和不确定性校准上均失败，固定审计门控拒绝了其强主张。本文提出的存档与裁决协议可重构公共评估历史、隔离验证的时间边界并证伪无依据的前沿主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共AI评测常被当作最终排行榜解读，但其背后的证据是受报告规则、基准修订和数据缺失影响的选择性时间序列，缺乏对其统计特性和可靠性的系统分析。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断框架，对多个公开存档（LiveBench、Open LLM Leaderboard v2、LMArena、GAIA、tau-bench）进行纵向分析；构建终端-历史对应模型；进行合成后验比较；提出存档与裁决协议。

Result: 固定报告惯例下，一个终端示例可兼容两种完全不同的历史路径（23.03 vs 75.13时间单位）；候选选择感知的前沿模型在合成恢复、客观存档预测、偏好迁移和不确定性校准方面均失败；固定审计门控拒绝了其更强的主张。

Conclusion: 公共AI评测需要存档与裁决协议来重构历史、验证时间界限并证伪无依据的主张，不能简单作为最终排行榜解读。

Abstract: Public AI evaluations are often read as terminal leaderboards, yet the underlying evidence is a selective time series shaped by reporting rules, benchmark revisions, and missingness. Repeated public archives for LiveBench and Open LLM Leaderboard v2 serve as the primary longitudinal record; LMArena provides a preference stress test; and GAIA and tau-bench contribute limited agentic pilots. Together, these archives instantiate a Bayesian inference problem: under a fixed reporting convention, one constructed terminal-only example over $1{,}000$ systems is compatible with two pre-terminal histories, yielding times of $23.03$ or $75.13$ to reach within $0.05$ of the ceiling under the same terminal-tail model. In synthetic posterior comparisons, action-facing diagnostics differ across observation regimes. The candidate selection-aware frontier model fails synthetic recovery, objective-archive prediction, preference transfer, and uncertainty calibration; correspondingly, fixed audit gates reject its stronger claims. An archive-and-adjudication protocol reconstructs public evaluation histories, isolates a verified timing boundary, and falsifies unsupported frontier claims.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Combining Retrieval-Augmented Text Generation with LLMs for Reading Content Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14817)
*Sooyeon Kim,Piotr S. Maciąg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合大语言模型(LLM)与检索增强生成(RAG)的个性化阅读内容生成系统，包含输入、RAG、生成和评判四个模块，支持用户指定问题和目标文本复杂度。实验表明RAG在所有模型和提示策略下均能提升系统性能，尤其是将相关性和 groundedness 最多提升26-35个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阅读内容生成系统难以同时满足用户对内容主题和文本复杂度的个性化需求，且生成内容往往缺乏事实依据。本文旨在通过LLM与RAG的结合，实现既个性化又具备事实支撑的阅读材料生成。

Method: 系统架构包含四个模块：(1) 输入模块，接收用户的问题和目标复杂度；(2) RAG模块，从互联网检索相关信息以丰富和夯实生成内容；(3) 生成模块，使用Meta LLaMA 4 Scout、LLaMA 3.1 8B Instant、Google Gemma2 9B三种LLM，结合Chain-of-Thought、零样本、少样本三种提示策略生成内容；(4) 评判模块，采用LLM-as-a-Judge自动评估答案质量和与目标可读性水平的匹配度。

Result: RAG持续提升了所有模型和提示策略下的系统性能，其中相关性和groundedness提升最为显著，最高达到26-35个百分点。RAG增强架构能够有效生成符合用户查询和期望文本复杂度的阅读内容。

Conclusion: RAG增强的架构能够有效生成符合用户查询和期望文本复杂度的个性化阅读内容，为智能教育、个性化学习等场景提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This work presents the design, implementation, and evaluation of a system for generating personalized reading content using Large Language Models (LLMs) combined with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). The proposed architecture consists of four modules: Input, RAG, Generation, and Judging and enables users to specify both a question and a target reading content complexity. RAG is employed to retrieve relevant information from the Internet, enriching and grounding the content produced by three modern LLMs: Meta LLaMA 4 Scout, LLaMA 3.1 8B Instant, and Google Gemma2 9B. Reading materials are generated using three prompting strategies (Chain-of-Thought, zero-shot, and few-shot), and the LLM-as-a-Judge module automatically evaluates answer quality and alignment with the desired readability level. Experimental results show that RAG consistently improves system performance across all models and prompting techniques, increasing relevance and particularly groundedness by up to 26-35 percentage points. Overall, the findings demonstrate that the RAG-augmented architecture effectively produces reading content tailored to user queries and desired textual complexity.

</details>


### [125] [Co-Scraper: query-aware DOM Pruning and Reusable Scraper Synthesis for Lightweight Web Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14821)
*Shoupeng Wang,Jiantao Qiu,Wuyang Zhang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Co-Scraper是一个两阶段框架，通过查询感知的DOM剪枝和稳定提取策略归纳，结合微调的Qwen3-8B模型，实现可复用的网络数据提取，在SWDE测试集上达到94.78%的F1分数和90.39%的复用成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络内容的海量异构特性需要自动化的信息提取，而生成可跨相似网页复用的爬虫是实现可扩展数据提取的有效方案。

Method: 提出Co-Scraper两阶段框架：1) 查询感知的DOM剪枝机制处理长HTML文档的层次复杂性；2) 稳定提取策略归纳，使用微调的Qwen3-8B模型将网页内容转化为可执行的程序化包装器。

Result: 在SWDE测试集上，F1分数达到94.78%，复用成功率为90.39%，均达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了数据提取的准确性和鲁棒性，为网络数据获取任务提供了一种高效方法。

Abstract: The abundant and heterogeneous nature of web content necessitates automated information extraction, and generating scrapers that can be reused across similar web pages offers an effective solution for scalable data extraction. In this work, we propose Co-Scraper, a two-stage framework capable of handling the hierarchical complexity of long HTML documents. By integrating a query-aware DOM pruning mechanism with stable extraction strategy induction, Co-Scraper can effectively transforms web content into executable programmatic wrappers using a fine-tuned Qwen3-8B model. On the test set of SWDE, Co-Scraper achieves state-of-the-art performance with an F1 score of 94.78% and a reuse success rate of 90.39%. This framework significantly enhances the accuracy and resilience of data extraction, providing a highly efficient approach for web data acquisition tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Retrieval-as-a-Service:A System-Oriented Analysis of Industrial Retrieval Pipelines in Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14932)
*Fang Liu,Yuan Yuan,Yifan Dang,Xuncheng Zhang,Cuiqianhe Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对工业检索系统进行了系统级调查，提出了统一检索即服务（RaaS）管道抽象，涵盖候选生成、语义匹配和重排序阶段，并分析了LLM集成的影响，为大规模Web系统提供了架构设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调查多聚焦算法进展，缺乏从基础设施视角分析生产环境下延迟、可扩展性和资源约束对检索系统设计影响的系统性研究。

Method: 提出统一RaaS管道抽象，将检索建模为多阶段服务（高效候选生成、嵌入语义匹配、资源感知重排序），并分析LLM检索机制对语义性能、延迟和计算开销的影响。

Result: 形成了对检索即服务基础设施的系统级理解，并提供了一系列实用设计指南。

Conclusion: 检索系统正从独立组件演变为面向服务的RaaS基础设施，LLM集成在提升语义性能的同时需平衡延迟与计算开销，研究为构建可扩展、高效且QoS感知的检索架构提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Retrieval systems have become a foundational infrastructure component in modern Web services, supporting applications such as content recommendation, advertising targeting, and API discovery. In large-scale industrial environments, retrieval is increasingly deployed as an independent service layer, commonly referred to as Retrieval-as-a-Service (RaaS). This paper presents a system-oriented survey of industrial retrieval pipelines, focusing on architectural design and deployment trade-offs under real-world constraints. Unlike prior surveys that emphasize algorithmic developments, we analyze retrieval systems from an infrastructure perspective, highlighting how latency requirements, scalability constraints, and resource limitations shape system design in production environments. We introduce a unified RaaS pipeline abstraction that models retrieval as a multi-stage service, including high-efficiency candidate generation, embedding-based semantic matching, and resource-aware re-ranking. We further examine the integration of Large Language Model (LLM)-based retrieval mechanisms and analyze their impact on semantic performance, latency, and computational overhead. The results provide a system-level understanding of retrieval as a service-oriented infrastructure and offer practical guidelines for designing scalable, efficient, and QoS-aware retrieval architectures in large-scale Web systems.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Positive Signals: Unlocking Implicit Negative Behaviors for Enhanced Sequential User Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15252)
*Zexuan Cheng,Yue Liu,Jun Zhang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出混合极性行为序列（Mixed-Polarity Behavior Sequences），将隐式负反馈（跳过、低参与度等）与正交互（点击、购买）共同编码到用户行为序列中，以替代传统的仅正交互序列，在多种CTR预测架构上实现+1.9%至+9.6%的相对AUC提升，并进一步提出目标感知极性融合（TAPF）机制解决语义不可区分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CTR预测中行为序列仅使用正交互（点击、购买等），而大量隐式负反馈（跳过、低参与度）被忽视；随着正序列长度收益递减，需要重新审视这一被低估的数据源。

Method: 1) 构建混合极性行为序列：在固定长度预算内按时间交错排列正负token；2) 提出Target-Aware Polarity Fusion (TAPF)：一种轻量级目标条件门控机制，用于区分不同极性行为的语义证据；3) 同时验证了更简单的极性偏置基线（polarity bias baseline）也能捕获大部分收益。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，跨越五种不同架构，混合极性序列带来+1.9%至+9.6%的相对AUC提升，且附加计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 混合极性数据范式本身是主要贡献来源，而非特定的融合机制；将负反馈纳入行为序列是提升CTR预测的有效且低成本手段。

Abstract: User behavior sequence modeling has become a central component in modern click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Over the past years, the community has invested substantial effort into improving how sequences are encoded, from target-aware attention and interest evolution networks to unified architectures that jointly process sequential and non-sequential features. However, a more fundamental question remains under-explored: what should constitute the behavior sequence? Current practice constructs sequences exclusively from positive interactions (clicks, purchases, completions), while the far more abundant implicit negative behaviors (skips, low engagement, scroll-past) are largely underutilized. As gains from longer positive sequences approach diminishing returns, we revisit this underutilized data source within the sequential modeling framework. In this paper, we demonstrate that mixed-polarity behavior sequences, which chronologically interleave positive and negative tokens within a fixed length budget, consistently outperform positive-only sequences across diverse model architectures with negligible additional computational overhead. We further identify a semantic indistinguishability problem inherent to naive polarity embeddings and propose Target-Aware Polarity Fusion (TAPF), a lightweight target-conditioned gating mechanism that provides additional gains by differentiating behavioral evidence. Notably, even the simpler polarity bias baseline captures the majority of improvement, underscoring that the primary contribution is the mixed-polarity data paradigm itself. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements of +1.9% to +9.6% relative AUC across five architectures, which validate the practical value of our approach.

</details>


### [128] [Guiding Federated Graph Recommendation with LLM-encoded knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15277)
*Thi Minh Chau Nguyen,Hien Trang Nguyen,Duc Anh Nguyen,Van Ho-Long,Thanh Trung Huynh,Zhao Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出利用大语言模型编码的语义知识来指导联邦图推荐中的结构表示聚合，解决非独立同分布客户端间图表示对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图推荐方法仅依赖结构聚合，忽略了跨客户端丰富的全局语义上下文，且非独立同分布数据导致局部学习的结构嵌入难以对齐。

Method: 客户端同时学习本地图结构表示，并通过冻结的大语言模型将典型交互模式压缩为紧凑语义向量；中央服务器利用这些语义信号发现跨客户端的相似偏好模式，从而指导结构表示的选择性聚合。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，利用大语言模型编码知识引导结构对齐一致性地提升了推荐准确率，优于现有联邦图基线方法。

Conclusion: 大语言模型编码的语义知识可以有效桥接联邦学习中的结构表示鸿沟，实现语义感知的跨客户端协作，同时不暴露原始数据。

Abstract: Graph-based recommender systems are highly effective at extracting collaborative signals from user--item interactions, and federated learning (FL) allows these models to be trained while preserving user privacy. However, aggregating graph representations across distributed, non-IID clients remains a challenge; structural embeddings learned locally often misalign, and naive averaging fails to capture meaningful cross-client relationships. Most existing federated graph methods rely exclusively on structural aggregation, neglecting the rich, global semantic context available in large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we propose a novel framework that uses LLM-encoded knowledge to guide federated graph recommendation. Specifically, clients learn structural representations from local graphs while simultaneously summarizing their typical interaction patterns into compact semantic vectors via a frozen LLM. The central server then uses these LLM-encoded semantic signals to discover related preference patterns across clients, guiding the selective aggregation of their structural representations. This enables semantically informed cross-client collaboration without exposing raw data. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks show that guiding structural alignment with LLM-encoded knowledge consistently improves recommendation accuracy over existing federated graph baselines.

</details>


### [129] [OneBar: An End-to-End Content-Grounded Generative Query Recommendation Framework for E-Commerce Video Feeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15330)
*Yao Tang,Ying Yang,Ben Chen,Yufei Ma,Zihan Liang,Chenyi Lei,Wenwu Ou,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneBar是一个端到端生成式框架，针对电商短视频平台实时查询推荐问题，通过协同多模态意图对齐、统一端到端架构与渐进式偏好学习，显著提升查询曝光、点击及GMV等业务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询推荐系统面临延迟约束与目标对齐问题，而生成式方法受限于内容侧元数据噪声与偏好漂移，亟需一种高效实时的端到端解决方案。

Method: (1) 协同多模态意图对齐模块：融合多模态视频理解与行为驱动的协同锚点；(2) 统一端到端架构 + 提示压缩机制，支持高效在线推理；(3) 渐进式偏好学习策略：将层次化行为偏好内化到生成策略中，无需单独训练奖励模型。

Result: 相比线上基线，Query曝光提升16.91%，点击提升18.68%，CTR略增0.19%；引导订单增加20.36%，GMV提升21.67%。

Conclusion: OneBar有效解决了短视频平台查询推荐的延迟与目标对齐问题，通过端到端生成式框架实现了业务指标的全面提升。

Abstract: Short-video platforms now expose clickable search entries beneath the video player, enabling users to easily express content-induced search intent. However, conventional query recommendation systems on short-video platforms suffer from latency constraints and objective misalignment, while recent generative approaches struggle with noisy content-side metadata and preference drift. To address these issues, we propose OneBar, an end-to-end generative framework for real-time query recommendation for E-Commerce video feeds. OneBar features three key innovations: (1) a collaborative-multimodal intent grounding module that fuses multimodal video understanding and behavior-derived collaborative anchors; (2) a Unified End-to-End architecture equipped with a prompt-compression mechanism for efficient online serving; and (3) a progressive preference learning strategy for efficient preference-internalization, which internalizes hierarchical behavior preferences into the generative policy, eliminating the need for a separately trained reward model. Compared with online base, OneBar increases Query Exposure by 16.91\% and Query Click by 18.68\%, while maintaining a slight Query CTR gain of 0.19\%. The additional search traffic further contributes to 20.36\% more guided orders and 21.67\% higher GMV.

</details>


### [130] [HoloRec: Holistic Encoding and Interleaved Reasoning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15331)
*Shuqi Zhao,Jingsong Su,Xiang Liu,Xingzhi Yao,Yiming Qiu,Huimu Wang,Liang Lin,Pengbo Mo,Mingming Li,Jiao Dai,Jizhong Han,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HoloRec提出了一种内生思维链推荐机制，通过多层次残差量化构建层次化语义编码矩阵，统一了表示、推理和生成。支持快速预测的非思考模式和自生成推理步骤的思考模式，在多个数据集上优于基线方法，尤其在稀疏场景下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型存在两个问题：1）缺乏用于多步推理的层次化语义结构；2）外部构建的思维链需要昂贵标注且与生成目标脱节。HoloRec旨在解决这些问题，实现表示、推理和生成的统一。

Method: 通过多粒度嵌套残差量化构建层次化语义编码矩阵，并使用整体重构损失进行优化。支持两种推理模式：非思考模式使用轻量级多粒度监督对齐进行快速预测；思考模式采用交错推理方案动态生成思维链步骤，将推理直接嵌入生成过程。

Result: 在多个公开推荐数据集上，HoloRec一致优于基线方法，在稀疏场景下增益尤为显著。思考模式比非思考模式取得了更好的准确率，且仅带来适度的推理开销。

Conclusion: HoloRec通过内生思维链机制有效统一了表示、推理和生成，无需外部标注数据即可实现层次化语义推理，在推荐任务上展现出优越性能，特别是在数据稀疏场景下优势明显。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models that formulate the task as sequence generation overcome the objective fragmentation problem of traditional cascade architectures, yet existing approaches still suffer from flat semantic representations lacking hierarchical structure for multi-step reasoning and an externally constructed chain-of-thought (CoT) that requires expensive annotations and remains disconnected from the generation objective. We propose HoloRec, an endogenous chain-of-thought recommendation mechanism that unifies representation, reasoning, and generation by constructing a hierarchical semantic encoding matrix via multi-granularity nested residual quantization optimized by a holistic reconstruction loss. HoloRec supports two inference modes: a non-thinking mode that uses lightweight multi-granularity supervised alignment for fast prediction, and a thinking mode that employs an interleaved reasoning scheme to generate CoT steps on the fly, directly embedding reasoning into the generation process without external data. Experiments on multiple public recommendation datasets demonstrate that HoloRec consistently outperforms baselines, with especially significant gains in sparse scenarios, and the thinking mode achieves better accuracy than the non-thinking mode with only modest inference overhead.

</details>


### [131] [Confidence-Based Stopping Methods for Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15380)
*Aaron Fletcher,Mark Stevenson*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出两种启发式停止方法，用于技术辅助审查中，通过监控已筛选文档是否包含足够信息来做出决策，而不是追求目标召回率。在诊断测试准确性系统评价的标准数据集上评估表明，这些方法大幅减少了需要审查的文档数量，同时在大多数情况下保持了与所有可用证据一致的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术辅助审查的停止方法主要关注达到目标召回率，而没有考虑信息需求是否已被满足。

Method: 提出了两种启发式停止方法，通过监控已筛选文档是否包含足够信息来做出决策。

Result: 在诊断测试准确性系统评价的数据集上，大幅减少了需要审查的文档数量，且在大多数情况下保持结论一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的停止方法能够在减少审查工作量的同时，维持与全部证据一致的结论。

Abstract: Technology Assisted Review stopping methods aim to ensure that no more documents are screened than necessary. Most existing approaches focus on achieving a target recall, which does not consider whether an information need has been met. This paper introduces two heuristic stopping methods that instead monitor whether screened documents contain enough information to make a decision. Evaluation on a standard dataset of Diagnostic Test Accuracy Systematic Reviews demonstrates that the proposed approaches substantially reduce the number of documents that need to be examined while, in the majority of cases, maintaining conclusions that are consistent with all evidence available.

</details>


### [132] [EventConnector: Mining Social Event Relations through Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15448)
*Zijie Lei,Haofei Yu,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EventConnector框架，通过构建时间事件图捕捉局部共波动和超前-滞后关系，并融合EC-Fusion自适应检索机制，在预测市场基准中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖语义相似性或全局时间序列对齐，忽略了真实世界事件之间常见的瞬态和方向性依赖关系，难以有效捕捉事件间的潜在关联。

Method: 构建时间事件图（EventConnector），基于时间序列轨迹捕捉事件的局部共波动和超前-滞后关系；提出EC-Fusion自适应检索机制，融合图结构得分与Granger因果信号，并通过图质量感知的混合权重进行动态平衡。

Result: 在Polymarket和Kalshi两个预测市场基准上，结合九种预测架构评估，EC-Fusion在17/18个模型-数据集指标中取得最优（非oracle方法），平均RMSE降低6.87%（最高达10.86%），经Holm-Bonferroni校正后p<0.01具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 基于时间图的建模结合因果信号融合，能有效捕捉超越语义相似性或传统对齐技术的事件潜在关系，验证了时间导向图建模在时序敏感应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Understanding and retrieving related real-world events based on their temporal dynamics is a fundamental challenge in time-sensitive applications such as forecasting, information retrieval, and social analysis. Existing methods often rely on semantic similarity or global time-series alignment, which overlook the transient and directional dependencies that frequently underlie real-world correlations. In this work, we introduce \textit{EventConnector}, a framework that constructs a temporal event graph capturing localized co-fluctuations and lead-lag relationships between events through their time-series trajectories. We further propose \textbf{EC-Fusion}, an adaptive retrieval mechanism that fuses EventConnector's graph-based scores with a complementary Granger-causal signal via a graph-quality-aware mixing weight. Across two real-world prediction market benchmarks (Polymarket and Kalshi) and nine forecasting architectures evaluated over three random seeds, EC-Fusion is the best non-oracle retrieval method on $17/18$ model--dataset cells, reducing RMSE by $6.87\%$ on average (up to $10.86\%$) over the strongest comparable retrieval baseline, with statistical significance at $p < 0.01$ after Holm--Bonferroni correction. These results highlight the effectiveness of temporally grounded graph modeling, augmented with causal-signal fusion, in capturing latent event relationships beyond what semantic similarity or traditional alignment techniques can offer.

</details>


### [133] [One Sequential Recommendation Model Pretrained from Synthetic Priors Predicts Multiple Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15752)
*Woosung Kang,Jiwon Jeong,Jonghyeok Shin,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SRPFN，一种基于先验数据拟合网络的序列推荐模型，通过在多样化合成序列数据上预训练，能够在目标域无需梯度更新即可进行零样本推理，并在多个基准上取得最优或次优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型依赖数据集特定训练，参数拟合到物品目录和交互分布，限制了跨域泛化能力，通常需要从头重新训练。

Method: SRPFN在合成先验数据（2560万序列）上离线预训练，学习生成后验预测分布；推理时仅需目标域的支持集（物品转换示例）进行条件化，无需梯度更新即可生成推荐。

Result: 在五个基准数据集、十个基线模型上，SRPFN在几乎所有指标上达到最优或次优性能，且计算效率显著高于训练基线。

Conclusion: 单一模型通过合成先验预训练即可泛化到多样化的真实世界域，为无需更新的序列推荐提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Existing sequential recommendation models rely on dataset-specific training, where the learned parameters are fitted to the item catalog and the observed interaction distribution of the training data. This limits generalization to new domains, typically requiring retraining from scratch. In this work, we propose SRPFN, a Prior-data Fitted Network for sequential recommendation -- predicting the next item in a single forward pass without any gradient-based parameter updates in the target domain. SRPFN is pretrained offline on 25.6M sequences sampled from a synthetic prior that spans diverse item-to-item transition patterns, learning to produce posterior predictive next-item distributions. At inference time, SRPFN generates recommendations by conditioning on a support set of item-item transition examples from the target domain, adapting to domain-specific patterns without retraining. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks across 10 baselines show that SRPFN achieves the best or second-best performance across nearly all metrics and datasets, while being substantially more computationally efficient than trained baselines. These results establish that a single model pretrained on synthetic priors can generalize across diverse real-world domains, offering a framework for update-free sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [134] [Intelligent Multimodal Retrieval and Reasoning for Geospatial Knowledge Discovery on the I-GUIDE Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15838)
*Yunfan Kang,Erick Li,Furqan Baig,Wei Hu,Alexander Michels,Anand Padmanabhan,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了I-GUIDE Smart Search，一个集成多模态检索、知识图谱和RAG的地理学空间知识发现系统，支持跨异构地理空间工件的语义检索、图谱遍历和对话式合成，在170个查询基准上相比基线方法显著提升了答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地理门户仅支持元数据和空间过滤，缺乏语义检索、图谱感知的溯源遍历和对话式合成能力，难以满足跨异构地理空间工件（数据集、地图、笔记本、软件、出版物及其溯源链接）的知识发现需求。

Method: 系统结合OpenSearch（关键词、向量、空间索引）、Neo4j知识图谱和迭代RAG流程，包含记忆感知查询增强、推理、检索方法路由、相关性评分、基于证据的生成、幻觉与相关性检查等模块。

Result: 在单A100部署下支持约100并发用户，每秒4.4请求，p50延迟约25秒（每次查询20-50次LLM调用）。在170个用户过滤查询和10个探针集上，相比非检索和朴素RAG基线，在精确标识符、空间约束、简单推荐和领域特定事实查询上提升最显著。

Conclusion: 总结了空间RAG系统的可迁移部署经验，涵盖空间元数据质量、图谱溯源、检索路由、接口契约、拒答感知评估、延迟-成本权衡以及用户界面在部署型地理空间网络基础设施中的作用。

Abstract: Geospatial knowledge discovery increasingly requires search across heterogeneous artifacts: datasets, maps, notebooks, software, publications, and the provenance links among them. Conventional geoportals support metadata and spatial filtering, but they rarely provide semantic retrieval, graph-aware provenance traversal, and conversational synthesis in one integrated system. This paper presents I-GUIDE Smart Search, a production multimodal geospatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system embedded in the I-GUIDE Platform, and reports on its design, deployment, and evaluation. The system combines production-maintained OpenSearch keyword, vector, and spatial indexes with a Neo4j knowledge graph and an iterative RAG pipeline for memory-aware query augmentation, reasoning, retrieval-method routing, relevance grading, grounded generation, hallucination and relevance checking. In a single-A100 RAG deployment, I-GUIDE Smart Search supports interactive use up to about 100 concurrent simulated users, reaching 4.4 requests per second with p50 latency near 25 seconds despite 20-50 LLM calls per query. For answer quality, we evaluate a four-category benchmark of 170 unique human-filtered user-facing queries, together with ten intent-specific probe sets generated from the deployed indexes and graph. Smart Search improves retrieved evidence coverage and judged answer quality over non-retrieval and naive-RAG baselines, with the clearest gains on exact-identifier, spatially constrained, simple-recommendation, and domain-specific factual queries requiring current indexed evidence. We distill transferable deployment lessons for spatial RAG systems, covering spatial metadata quality, graph provenance, retrieval routing, interface contracts, refusal-aware evaluation, latency-cost tradeoffs, and the role of the user interface in deployed geospatial cyberinfrastructure.

</details>


### [135] [MAGE-RAG: Multigranular Adaptive Graph Evidence for Agentic Multimodal RAG in Long-Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15906)
*Yilong Zuo,Xunkai Li,Jing Yuan,Qiangqiang Dai,Hongchao Qin,Ronghua Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MAGE-RAG提出了一种多粒度自适应图证据框架，通过离线构建包含页面节点和元素节点的证据图，在线迭代激活、打开、搜索和剪枝证据子图，为长文档多模态问答提供紧凑且相关的结构化输入，在LongDocURL和MMLongBench-Doc上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法存在静态权衡问题：文本检索丢失视觉和布局信息，页面级视觉检索引入大量无关噪声。亟需一种能动态平衡证据覆盖、噪声控制和推理成本的方法。

Method: 离线构建多粒度证据图（页面节点+元素节点），编码包含关系、阅读顺序、布局邻接、章节层级和语义邻居关系；在线证据控制器在显式预算下迭代激活、打开、搜索和剪枝证据子图，并将其渲染为结构化多模态输入供LVLM消费。

Result: LongDocURL上52.75总体准确率，MMLongBench-Doc上53.26准确率与51.19 F1。细粒度分解、预算-性能曲线、消融和轨迹分析验证了查询时证据子图构建能有效平衡分散证据覆盖与上下文噪声控制。

Conclusion: MAGE-RAG通过多粒度自适应图证据机制有效解决了长文档多模态问答中证据稀疏、模态异构和布局复杂的挑战，实现了证据覆盖与噪声控制之间的动态平衡。

Abstract: Long-document multimodal question answering requires a system to locate sparse evidence in long PDFs and integrate clues from text, tables, images, charts, and complex layouts. Existing RAG methods mostly rely on fixed Top-k retrieval over text chunks or pages. Text retrieval can compress the context but often loses visual and layout information; page-level visual retrieval preserves the original page, yet it also sends large irrelevant regions to the reader, leading to a static trade-off among evidence coverage, noise, and inference cost. This paper proposes MAGE-RAG, a multigranular adaptive graph evidence framework for long-document multimodal QA. MAGE-RAG uses page retrieval as the entry point for query-time evidence construction. Offline, it builds an evidence graph with page nodes and element nodes, encoding containment, reading order, layout adjacency, section hierarchy, and semantic-neighbor relations. At query time, an online evidence controller iteratively activates, opens, searches, and prunes evidence under explicit budgets. The resulting evidence subgraph is then rendered into structured multimodal reader input, allowing the LVLM to consume compact and relevant evidence within a limited context. On LongDocURL and MMLongBench-Doc, we establish a unified comparison and analysis protocol covering Direct MLLM, Text RAG, Page-level Visual RAG, and Graph/Agentic RAG. Experiments show that MAGE-RAG achieves 52.75 overall accuracy on LongDocURL, and 53.26 accuracy with 51.19 F1 on MMLongBench-Doc. Fine-grained breakdowns, budget-performance curves, ablations, and trace-based analysis further show that query-time evidence subgraph construction can balance dispersed evidence coverage with context-noise control. Our code is available at https://github.com/laonuo2004/MAGE-RAG.git.

</details>


### [136] [Entity Labels Are Not Entity Signals: A Framework for Observable Relevance in Document Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15998)
*Utshab Kumar Ghosh,Shubham Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了概念实体相关性（CER）与可观测实体相关性（OER）的区分，指出传统实体感知检索假设语义相关的实体即为有效检索信号是不充分的。实验表明CER与OER一致性近乎随机（κ≈0），而基于OER的监督可将非相关文档剪枝率提升10倍，开放世界MAP提升0.051。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体感知文档检索通常假设与查询语义相关的实体也能作为有效的排序信号，本文指出这一假设存在根本缺陷——实体链接是 imperfect linker 产生的假设而非真实观测，一个实体可能在主题上相关但缺乏区分性信号。

Method: 形式化定义了CER（概念实体相关性）和OER（可观测实体相关性），在四个数据集上使用人工实体标注和多种OER操作化方案进行对比实验，通过Cohen's κ系数衡量一致性。

Result: CER与OER之间κ≈0（近乎随机一致），而OER操作化方案之间κ≈0.5（高度一致）；CER监督仅能剔除不到4%的非相关文档，OER监督将非相关文档剪枝率提升10倍，开放世界MAP较BM25提升0.051。

Conclusion: 实体感知检索应从概念相关性（实体是否与主题相关）转向可观测相关性（实体是否在集合中区分相关与非相关文档），以提升检索效果。

Abstract: Entity-aware document retrieval uses query-associated entities as ranking signals, assuming that semantically relevant entities are also useful retrieval signals. We show this assumption is insufficient- and explain why. Unlike terms, which are ground-truth observations, entity links are hypotheses produced by an imperfect linker: an entity can be topically central yet provide no discriminative signal if the linker fires indiscriminately across relevant and non-relevant documents. We formalize this as a distinction between Conceptual Entity Relevance (CER)- whether an entity is topically related to a query- and Observable Entity Relevance (OER)- whether its observed presence in a collection discriminates relevant from non-relevant documents. Across four collections and annotation sources including human entity judgments, CER and OER exhibit near-chance agreement ($κ\approx 0$), while OER operationalizations agree substantially ($κ\approx 0.5$), confirming CER as the systematic outlier. CER-based supervision selects topically plausible but weakly discriminative entities, pruning fewer than 4% of non-relevant documents on some collections. Aligning supervision with OER improves non-relevant pruning by up to 10x and open-world MAP by 0.051 over BM25. Our findings motivate a shift from conceptual to observable notions of entity relevance in entity-aware retrieval.

</details>


### [137] [Theorem-Grounded Execution Ontologies for Interpretable Machine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16010)
*Raghu Anantharangachar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出定理驱动执行本体(TGEO)框架，将推理建模为可执行的状态转换过程而非文本生成序列，通过显式表示每个状态转换、操作应用和验证步骤，实现可解释、可重放和可审计的推理表示，在定理密集型推理任务上展示了可执行推理表示对可解释、可验证和可复现AI推理系统的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的推理过程是潜在(latent)的，难以解释、验证、回放、调试和跨域迁移。现有的链式思维、树状思维、图状思维和工具增强推理等方法暴露了中间推理产物，但缺乏显式的执行语义、形式化状态表示和可验证的推理结构。

Method: TGEO将推理建模为可执行的状态转换过程。给定输入问题，TGEO识别相关定理族，将问题绑定到领域本体，发现语义对象，实例化状态和操作符，构建谓词和契约，并合成可执行的推理图。框架包含五个架构组件：(1)定理驱动的推理先验、(2)可执行本体、(3)操作中介的状态转换、(4)基于谓词和契约的执行验证、(5)架构审计与故障定位。

Result: 在来自数学基准领域的定理密集型推理任务和一个精心策划的Golden Execution Suite上进行了评估。结果表明可执行推理表示对于构建可解释、可验证和可复现的AI推理系统具有价值。

Conclusion: TGEO通过将推理过程从隐式序列转变为显式可执行状态转换图，显著提升了推理的可解释性、可验证性和可复现性，为构建更可靠的AI推理系统提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved impressive performance on reasoning tasks spanning mathematics, science, programming, and commonsense inference. Despite these advances, their reasoning processes remain largely latent, making them difficult to interpret, verify, replay, debug, and transfer across domains. Existing approaches such as chain-of-thought, tree-of-thoughts, graph-of-thoughts, and tool-augmented reasoning expose intermediate reasoning artifacts but typically lack explicit execution semantics, formal state representations, and verifiable reasoning structures.
  We introduce Theorem-Grounded Execution Ontologies (TGEO), a framework that models reasoning as an executable state-transition process rather than a sequence of generated tokens. Given an input problem, TGEO identifies relevant theorem families, binds the problem to a domain ontology, discovers semantic objects, instantiates states and operators, constructs predicates and contracts, and synthesizes an executable reasoning graph. The resulting graph provides an interpretable, replayable, and auditable representation of reasoning in which every state transition, operator application, and validation step is explicitly represented.
  TGEO integrates five architectural components: (1) theorem-grounded reasoning priors, (2) executable ontologies, (3) operator-mediated state transitions, (4) predicate and contract-based execution validation, and (5) architectural auditing and failure localization.
  We evaluate TGEO on theorem-intensive reasoning tasks derived from mathematical benchmark domains and a curated Golden Execution Suite. Our findings demonstrate the value of executable reasoning representations for interpretable, verifiable, and reproducible AI reasoning systems.

</details>


### [138] [RL-Index: Reinforcement Learning for Retrieval Index Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16316)
*Yongjia Lei,Nedim Lipka,Zhisheng Qi,Utkarsh Sahu,Koustava Goswami,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RL-Index框架，将检索索引推理建模为强化学习问题，通过在索引阶段用LLM生成的理由增强文档，将推理从查询阶段转移到索引阶段，使用GRPO和检索相似度作为奖励信号优化理由质量，从而提升检索和下游QA性能并降低在线延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法依赖查询端推理（如查询重写），面临在线延迟高的问题，且未充分利用在知识语料库本身进行推理（索引端推理）的机会，难以处理查询与知识之间需要隐式复杂推理的匹配场景。

Method: 提出RL-Index智能索引框架，将检索索引推理建模为强化学习问题；在索引阶段用LLM生成的理由增强文档，显式编码潜在查询-知识关系；采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 优化理由质量，以检索相似度作为可验证的奖励信号，直接优化索引决策以提升检索效果。

Result: 在BRIGHT基准上，RL-Index持续提升检索和下游问答性能，同时显著降低在线推理延迟；学习的理由增强可泛化至多种检索器和生成器，展现出跨检索系统的即插即用鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将推理从查询端转移到索引端是有效的策略，RL-Index通过强化学习优化索引端的理由增强，能够在不增加在线延迟的前提下提升复杂知识检索的效果，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Retrieving external knowledge is essential for solving real-world tasks, yet it remains challenging when the relationship between a query and its relevant knowledge involves implicit and complex reasoning beyond surface-level semantic or lexical matching (e.g., mathematical problems relying on the same theorem or coding requiring deep reasoning). Existing approaches primarily rely on query-side reasoning (e.g., query rewriting), which introduces significant online latency and underutilizes the opportunity to perform reasoning over the knowledge corpus itself (i.e., index-side reasoning). In this paper, we propose RL-Index, an agentic indexing framework that formulates retrieval index reasoning as a reinforcement learning problem. Instead of performing reasoning at query time, RL-Index shifts reasoning to the indexing stage by augmenting documents with LLM-generated rationales that explicitly encode the latent query-knowledge relationship. To optimize the quality of these rationales, we employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and use retrieval similarity as a verifiable reward signal, enabling direct optimization of indexing decisions for retrieval effectiveness. Extensive experiments on the BRIGHT benchmark demonstrate that RL-Index consistently improves both retrieval and downstream question-answering performance, while significantly reducing online inference latency. Moreover, the learned rationale augmentation generalizes across diverse retrievers and generators, highlighting its robustness as a plug-and-play indexing strategy across different retrieval systems.

</details>


### [139] [Leveraging Code-Mixed Product Metadata and User Feedback for Personalized Recommendation on Daraz Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16387)
*KM Fahim A Bari,Muhammad Abdullah Adnan,Nafis Sadeq*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文首次对孟加拉语、英语及Banglish（孟加拉语音译拉丁化）混合语料的电商评论进行推荐系统基准测试，发现Banglish词汇碎片化导致NDCG@10下降46.8%，并系统性评估了六类推荐模型在不同数据密度下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语料（Code-mixed）评论对推荐系统性能的影响尚未被充分探索，特别是在低资源语种（如孟加拉语及其拉丁音译变体Banglish）的电商场景中。

Method: 使用Daraz孟加拉平台的产品评论数据，采用per-user chronological leave-last-out协议，评估六类推荐模型；通过k-core阈值消融实验调控数据密度（59.3%用户仅有一条交互）；对内容过滤模型进行语言分层评估，使用字符n-gram TF-IDF刻画Banglish词汇碎片化影响。

Result: Item-based CF在不同密度下表现稳定；Implicit MF随密度降低性能急剧下降；Explicit MF在高阈值下表现提升。Banglish词汇碎片化导致NDCG@10比孟加拉语用户低46.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合语码、低资源电商场景下的推荐研究建立了可复现的评估基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Bangladeshi e-commerce platforms host millions of product reviews written in Bengali Unicode, English, and Banglish, where Bengali is phonetically transcribed in Latin script. However, the impact of code-mixed reviews on recommendation performance remains largely unexplored. We present the first such benchmarking on product reviews from Daraz Bangladesh, evaluating six model families under a per-user chronological leave-last-out protocol. To address the severe long-tail sparsity of the dataset, where 59.3% of users have exactly one interaction, we conduct a systematic k-core threshold ablation across five density configurations. The results reveal that Item-based Collaborative Filtering remains stable across settings, Implicit Matrix Factorization degrades sharply with decreasing density, and Explicit Matrix Factorization uniquely improves at higher thresholds. To characterize the impact of code-mixing on recommendation quality, we perform a language-stratified evaluation of content-based filtering using character n-gram TF-IDF profiles. The results provide empirical evidence that fragmentation of the Banglish vocabulary reduces NDCG@10 by 46.8% relative to Bengali-script users, a degradation traceable to transliteration inconsistency across surface forms. This work establishes a reproducible evaluation foundation for recommendation research in code-mixed, low-resource e-commerce settings. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/os-car-war-thy/daraz-recsys.

</details>


### [140] [PIANO: Personalized Reranking via Information Aggregation Node for Music Search Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16641)
*Weisheng Li,Chuqiao Huang,Pengcheng Li,Zhengchao Peng,Qiang Xiao,Zhongqian Xie,Qiang Huang,Chuanjiang Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PIANO是一个针对音乐搜索场景的个性化列表级重排序框架，通过查询驱动兴趣精炼器（QDIR）利用历史查询对齐用户意图，以及信息聚合节点（IAN）实现列表级别的CTR/CVR联合优化，在网易云音乐上实现了CTR+0.62%和CVR+4.45%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐track具有长生命周期和持久价值，其搜索重排序需要同时对齐用户当前查询与长期偏好，并联合优化CTR和CVR。现有方法存在两个局限：(1) 序列方法依赖物品交互历史，无法利用历史搜索查询来匹配当前搜索意图；(2) 多数列表级模型只优化单一目标（如仅CTR），传统多目标方法在物品层面平衡点击和转化，忽视了整个排序列表中的权衡。

Method: 提出PIANO框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 查询驱动兴趣精炼器（QDIR），通过跨注意力机制对历史查询进行建模，将过去意图与当前查询对齐；(ii) 信息聚合节点（IAN），一个可学习的[CLS]风格token，聚合候选列表并在列表级别预测CTR/CVR。

Result: 在公开数据集和工业数据集上的大量实验表明，PIANO相比强基线取得了一致性提升。在网易云音乐在线A/B测试中，CTR提升0.62%，CVR提升4.45%，均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: PIANO通过利用历史查询意图和列表级多目标优化，有效解决了音乐搜索重排序中个性化对齐和联合优化的问题。

Abstract: Unlike short-video content, music tracks have long lifecycles and lasting value. Effective music search re-ranking must therefore align the user's current query with long-term preferences while jointly optimizing Click-Through Rate (CTR) and Conversion Rate (CVR). However, existing methods suffer from two limitations: (1) sequential methods rely on item-interaction history and therefore cannot use historical search queries to tell which past preferences match the user's current search intent; (2) most listwise models optimize a single objective (e.g., CTR only), and conventional multi-objective methods balance click and conversion at the item level, ignoring how these trade-offs play out across the whole ranked list. To address these limitations, we propose PIANO, a personalized listwise re-ranking framework with two key components: (i) the Query-Driven Interest Refiner (QDIR) uses cross-attention over historical queries to align past intents with the current one; (ii) the Information Aggregation Node (IAN), a learnable [CLS]-style token, aggregates the candidate list and predicts CTR/CVR at the list level. Extensive experiments on public and industrial datasets show consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests on NetEase Cloud Music, a leading music streaming platform, PIANO achieves statistically significant improvements in CTR (+0.62%) and CVR (+4.45%).

</details>


### [141] [SCAR: Semantic Continuity-Aware Retrieval for Efficient Context Expansion in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16661)
*Nathanaël Langlois*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCAR是一种自适应检索策略，通过选择性扩展相邻块来缓解RAG中的边界碎片化问题，在边界碎片查询上达到92.8%召回率，同时比静态窗口减少22.9%的chunk数量，并跨嵌入模型迁移无需重新校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定长度分块在RAG中常导致边界碎片化，关键证据被分割到不同片段，降低检索召回率；现有静态窗口和父检索方法虽提升召回但引入显著token开销。

Method: SCAR提出自适应检索策略，通过权衡查询-邻居相关性与结构连续性惩罚来选择性扩展相邻块；采用相对扩展阈值（基于每个检索块自身的查询相关性），形成近似尺度不变的决策规则，可在不同嵌入模型间迁移而无需重新校准。

Result: 在四个语料库（RFC、GDPR、10-K报告、合并协议；N=320查询；160个边界碎片查询）上，SCAR在边界碎片查询上达到92.8%召回率，仅用7.84个chunk，比静态窗口（10.16个chunk）减少22.9%；配对bootstrap检验（B=10,000）确认chunk减少显著（p<0.0001, Cohen's d=-1.49），召回差异较小（Cohen's d=-0.33）。在三个嵌入模型上使用同一超参数设置可迁移，在10-K语料上RAGAS评估表明生成忠实度不变且上下文token减少27.1%。

Conclusion: SCAR在有效缓解边界碎片化问题的同时显著降低token开销，且具有良好的跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Fixed-length chunking in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often leads to boundary fragmentation, where critical evidence is split across segments, degrading retrieval recall. While static windowing and parent retrieval improve recall, they introduce significant token overhead. We propose SCAR (Semantic Continuity-Aware Retrieval), an adaptive retrieval policy that selectively expands neighboring chunks by weighing query-neighbor relevance against a structural continuity penalty. SCAR uses a relative expansion threshold tied to each retrieved chunk's own query-relevance, yielding an approximately scale-invariant decision rule that transfers across embedding models without recalibration. Across four diverse corpora (RFC, GDPR, a 10-K report, and a Merger agreement; N=320 queries; 160 boundary-fragmented), SCAR achieves 92.8% recall on boundary-fragmented queries with only 7.84 chunks, a 22.9% reduction compared to static windowing (10.16 chunks). Paired bootstrap tests (B=10,000) confirm the chunk reduction is highly significant (p<0.0001, Cohen's d=-1.49, large effect), with a small recall difference (Cohen's d=-0.33). The policy transfers across three embedding models (text-embedding-3-large, BGE-large-en-v1.5, zembed-1) using the same single hyperparameter setting, and downstream RAGAS evaluation on the 10-K corpus confirms SCAR preserves generation faithfulness while reducing context tokens by 27.1%.

</details>


### [142] [Harmonizing Semantic and Collaborative in LLMs: Reasoning-based Embedding Generator for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16703)
*Qidong Liu,Mingyao Huang,Moranxin Wang,Wenxuan Yang,Haiping Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReaEmb通过潜在推理增强对比学习（LRCL）和协作奖励强化学习（CRRL）两阶段框架，利用LLM的内在推理能力并显式注入协作信号，解决了基于LLM的序列推荐系统中语义推理不足和协作对齐缺乏显式指导的问题，在三个数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的序列推荐嵌入方法存在两个根本性缺陷：一是未能充分利用LLM的内部推理能力，二是通过监督微调隐式注入协作信号，缺乏对协作嵌入对齐的显式指导。这两个缺陷导致长尾问题仍然存在。

Method: 提出ReaEmb框架，包含两个阶段：(1) LRCL（潜在推理增强对比学习）阶段，通过两轮前向传播和额外注意力模块挖掘LLM的内在推理能力；(2) CRRL（协作奖励强化学习）阶段，通过定制的强化学习机制显式地将协作信号注入LLM。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，ReaEmb在多种SRS模型上均表现出优越的有效性。

Conclusion: ReaEmb通过结合LLM的内在推理能力与显式协作信号注入，有效提升了序列推荐系统的性能，代码已开源以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Sequential Recommender Systems (SRS) predict the next item of interest based on users' interaction histories and have been widely deployed, but hindered by long-tail problem. Large Language Models (LLMs), with strong semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities, offer a promising way to enrich item semantics and have recently been used as embedding generators. However, two fundamental gaps remain. First, current LLM-based embedding methods fail to exploit the model's inner reasoning capacity.
